await rr.text() : \"\";\n      const lines = t.split(\"\\n\");\n      const infoRows = lines.filter(l => /params|context length|context window|activated|precision/i.test(l) && l.includes(\"|\")).slice(0, 6);\n      const ctxHits = (t.match(/[^\\n]{0,60}context length[^\\n]{0,90}/gi) || []).slice(0, 2);\n      // 取第一个基准表格块\n      let bench = [];\n      const idx = lines.findIndex(l => /^#{2,4}\\s.*(bench|evaluat|perf)/i.test(l));\n      if (idx >= 0) bench = lines.slice(idx, idx + 26).filter(l => benchRe.test(l) && /\\d/.test(l)).slice(0, 9);\n      const relHint = (t.match(/[^\\n]{0,100}(release|released on|published)[^\\n]{0,100}/gi) || []).slice(0, 2);\n      out.push({\n        id,\n        createdAt: j.createdAt,\n        license: card.license + (card.license_name ?   "text": "#### MiniMax-M3\n\nCollection\n\n7 items • Updated Aug 15 • 24\n\n [![Hugging Face's logo](https://huggingface.co/front/assets/huggingface_logo-noborder.svg) Hugging Face](https://huggingface.co/)\n\nMiniMax-M3 is a native multimodal model with 1M context. It has ~428B parameters and ~23B activated parameters.\n\n**Highlights:**\n\n-   **Native Multimodality:** M3 undergoes mixed-modality training from the very first step, enabling deeper semantic fusion across text, image, and video.\n-   **Context Scaling via Sparse Attention:** M3 introduces MiniMax Sparse Attention (MSA) to improve long context efficiency. M3 delivers 9× prefill and 15× decode speedups compared to M2 at 1M context, reducing per-token compute to 1/20.\n-   **Coding & Cowork Capability:** M3 achieves frontier-level performance across long-horizon agentic benchmarks, excelling in both coding and cowork.\n\nM3 is powered by [**MiniMax Sparse Attention (MSA)**](https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/MSA), a high-performance sparse attention operator designed for million-token contexts. Compared with GQA, MSA dramatically reduces the attention compute and memory footprint while preserving model quality.\n\n> 📄 Read the technical report: [arXiv:2606.13392](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13392) · [Hugging Face Papers](https://huggingface.co/papers/2606.13392)\n\nM3 supports three reasoning modes through the `thinking` parameter:\n\n`enabled``adaptive``disabled`\n\nDownload the model:\n\n```bash\nhf download MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-M3 --local-dir MiniMax-M3\n```\n\nWe recommend the following inference frameworks to serve the model:\n\n[SGLang](https://docs.sglang.io/) - see [SGLang cookbook](https://docs.sglang.io/cookbook/autoregressive/MiniMax/MiniMax-M3).\n\n[vLLM](https://github.com/vllm-project/vllm) - see [vLLM recipes](https://recipes.vllm.ai/MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-M3).\n\n[Transformers](https://github.com/huggingface/transformers) - see [Transformers docs](https://huggingface.co/docs/transformers/model_doc/minimax_m3_vl).\n\n[KTransformers](https://github.com/kvcache-ai/ktransformers) - see [KTransformers MiniMax-M3 tutorial](https://github.com/kvcache-ai/ktransformers/blob/main/doc/en/kt-kernel/MiniMax-M3-Tutorial.md).\n\n[unsloth](https://unsloth.ai/) - see [tutorial](https://unsloth.ai/docs/models/minimax-m3)\n\n[ATOM](https://github.com/ROCm/ATOM/tree/main) - see [MiniMax-M3 MXFP4/MXFP8 Usage Guide](https://github.com/ROCm/ATOM/blob/main/recipes/MiniMax-M3.md)\n\nWe recommend the following parameters for best performance: `temperature=1.0`, `top_p=0.95`.\n\nContact us at [model@minimax.io](mailto:model@minimax.io).\n\nDownloads last month\n\n194,557\n\n59 \\*",

{
  "title": "MiniMax Sparse Attention",
  "url": "https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13392",
  "mode": "article",
  "sourceSelector": "body > div.flex-wrap-footer:nth-of-type(2) > main",
  "byline": "[Submitted on 11 Jun 2026 (v1), last revised 12 Jun 2026 (this version, v2)]",
  "siteName": "arXiv.org",
  "publishedTime": "",
  "excerpt": "Ultra-long-context capability is becoming indispensable for frontier LLMs: agentic workflows, repository-scale code reasoning, and persistent memory all require the model to jointly attend over hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens, yet the quadratic cost of softmax attention makes this untenable at deployment scale. We introduce MiniMax Sparse Attention (MSA), a blockwise sparse attention built upon Grouped Query Attention (GQA). A lightweight Index Branch scores key-value blocks and independently selects a Top-k subset for each GQA group, enabling group-specific sparse retrieval while maintaining efficient block-level execution; the Main Branch then performs exact block-sparse attention over only the selected blocks. Designed around a principle of simplicity and scalability, MSA is deliberately streamlined, making it straightforward to deploy efficiently across a broad range of GPUs. To translate sparsity into practical speedups, we co-design MSA with a GPU execution path that uses exp-free Top-k selection and KV-outer sparse attention to improve tensor-core utilization under block-granular access. On a 109B-parameter model with native multimodal training, MSA performs on par with GQA while reducing per-token attention compute by 28.4x at 1M context. Paired with our co-designed kernel, MSA achieves 14.2x prefill and 7.6x decoding wall-clock speedups on H800. Our inference kernel is available at: https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/MSA. A production-grade natively multimodal model powered by MSA has been publicly released at: https://huggingface.co/MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-M3.",
  "text": "[Xunhao Lai](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Lai,+X), [Weiqi Xu](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Xu,+W), [Yufeng Yang](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Yang,+Y), [Qiaorui Chen](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Chen,+Q), [Yang Xu](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Xu,+Y), [Lunbin Zeng](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Zeng,+L), [Xiaolong Li](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Li,+X), [Haohai Sun](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Sun,+H), [Haichao Zhu](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Zhu,+H), [Vito Zhang](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Zhang,+V), [Jinkai Hu](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Hu,+J), [Jiayao Li](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Li,+J), [Rui Gao](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Gao,+R), [Zekun Li](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Li,+Z), [Songquan Zhu](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Zhu,+S), [Jingkai Zhou](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Zhou,+J), [Pengyu Zhao](https://arxiv.org/search/cs?searchtype=author&query=Zhao,+P)\n\n> Ultra-long-context capability is becoming indispensable for frontier LLMs: agentic workflows, repository-scale code reasoning, and persistent memory all require the model to jointly attend over hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens, yet the quadratic cost of softmax attention makes this untenable at deployment scale. We introduce MiniMax Sparse Attention (MSA), a blockwise sparse attention built upon Grouped Query Attention (GQA). A lightweight Index Branch scores key-value blocks and independently selects a Top-k subset for each GQA group, enabling group-specific sparse retrieval while maintaining efficient block-level execution; the Main Branch then performs exact block-sparse attention over only the selected blocks. Designed around a principle of simplicity and scalability, MSA is deliberately streamlined, making it straightforward to deploy efficiently across a broad range of GPUs. To translate sparsity into practical speedups, we co-design MSA with a GPU execution path that uses exp-free Top-k selection and KV-outer sparse attention to improve tensor-core utilization under block-granular access. On a 109B-parameter model with native multimodal training, MSA performs on par with GQA while reducing per-token attention compute by 28.4x at 1M context. Paired with our co-designed kernel, MSA achieves 14.2x prefill and 7.6x decoding wall-clock speedups on H800. Our inference kernel is available at: [this https URL](https://github.com/MiniMax-AI/MSA). A production-grade natively multimodal model powered by MSA has been publicly released at: [this https URL](https://huggingface.co/MiniMaxAI/MiniMax-M3).\n\n<table><tbody><tr><td>Comments:</td><td>30 pages, 14 figures</td></tr><tr><td>Subjects:</td><td><span>Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)</span></td></tr><tr><td>Cite as:</td><td><span><a href=\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13392\">arXiv:2606.13392</a> [cs.AI]</span></td></tr><tr><td>&nbsp;</td><td>(or <span><a href=\"https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13392v2\">arXiv:2606.13392v2</a> [cs.AI]</span> for this version)</td></tr><tr><td>&nbsp;</td><td><a href=\"https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2606.13392\">https://doi.org/10.48550/arXiv.2606.13392</a></td></tr></tbody></table>\n\n## Submission history\n\n",

最近 6 个月（2026-04-18 至 2026-10-18）主流开源权重大模型对比

以下 9 个模型均可在 Hugging Face 直接下载权重，字段全部取自各自官方模型卡（发布日期按权重上线日；Kimi K3 例外，已单独标注）。

关键观察

  • 1M 上下文成为旗舰标配：除 Qwen3.8 系列仍以 262K 原生、RoPE 外扩到 1M 外，DeepSeek V4 全系、Kimi K3、GLM-5.2/5.3 的配置和模型卡都直接支持 1M。
  • 协议分化：DeepSeek 与 GLM-5.2 放开到 MIT，仅 Qwen3.8-27B 保持 Apache-2.0；三个最新旗舰（Kimi K3、GLM-5.3、Qwen3.8-Max）改用自定义开放权重协议，商用需逐条看条款。
  • 竞争重心已从知识问答转向 agentic 与编码：同为“旗舰”档，Terminal-Bench 2.1 从 81.0（GLM-5.2）提升到 90.6（DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash）；DeepSWE 从 46.2 提升到 74.2。同期 GPQA-Diamond 只在 88 到 93.5 之间小幅波动，说明知识类基准已接近饱和。
  • 开源模型分为稠密小模型和超大 MoE 规模：一端是 Qwen3.8-27B（Apache-2.0、单机可跑），另一端是 2.4T、2.78T 的 MoE，中间有 125B/6B 这类“小激活高吞吐”档。

计算标准与限制

  • 发布日基本取 Hugging Face 权重仓库的创建日（也是官方模型卡所在处）；Kimi K3 的仓库建得更早（6-13），模型卡基准标注为 “as of 2026-07-16/23”，因此按 7-16 记录，存在几天误差。
  • GLM-5.2 的 GPQA 91.2 来自 Kimi K3 模型卡的横向对照表，并非 GLM-5.2 自己的表；其余分数均取自各自模型卡。
  • 同一家族只列代表作，未重复列出同一模型的 FP8/BF16 和底座版本；DeepSeek-V4-Pro 的 Codeforces 3206 与 V4.1 卡上的 3348 属不同评测设置，已按各模型卡原文记录。
  • 未纳入：Meta（meta-llama 最新仍是 Llama 4，2025-04）、OpenAI（最新仍是 gpt-oss，2025-08）在窗口内没有新的开源权重；Google Gemma 4（2026-03-11）与 Mistral Medium 3.5 128B（2026-03-31）早于 6 个月窗口；MiniMax-M3（2026-06，约 428B、23B 激活、1M 上下文、MiniMax Community 协议）的基准只以图片形式放在模型卡上，无可用文本分数，因此未列入表内。

已留存的数据

Data 表 开源大模型对比_2026H2：9 行，字段为 模型、发布方、发布日期、参数量、上下文长度、协议、主要基准成绩、备注、官方来源（按模型名去重）。

It is the world's first open 3T-class model, designed for frontier intelligence across long-horizon coding, knowledge work, and reasoning.\n\nWhile its overall performance still trails the most powerful proprietary models, Claude Fable 5 and GPT 5.6 Sol, Kimi K3 demonstrated frontier-level performance across our evaluation suite, consistently outperforming other tested models.\n\nKimi K3 is available today on [Kimi.ai](https://www.kimi.ai/), [Kimi Work](https://www.kimi.ai/products/kimi-work), [Kimi Code](https://www.kimi.ai/code), and the [Kimi API](https://platform.kimi.ai/). The full model weights will be released by July 27, 2026. Further details on the architecture, training, and evaluations will be released alongside the Kimi K3 technical report.\n\n## An Open 3T-Class Model\n\nKimi K3 is the first open model to reach 2.8 trillion parameters. It marks the latest step in Kimi's sustained push at the scaling frontier: for nine of the past twelve months, Kimi models have set the upper bound of open-model sizes.\n\nKimi K3 is built on Kimi Delta Attention (KDA) and Attention Residuals (AttnRes), two architectural updates designed to improve how information flows across sequence length and model depth. We have also scaled up Mixture of Experts (MoE) sparsity, effectively activating 16 out of 896 experts when paired with a Stable LatentMoE framework. Together with refined training and data recipes, these structural changes yield an approximate 2.5× improvement in overall scaling efficiency compared to Kimi K2, allowing the model to convert compute into intelligence more effectively.\n\n## Coding\n\nKimi K3 has strong long-horizon coding performance. Operating with minimal human oversight, it can sustain long engineering sessions, navigate massive repositories, and orchestrate terminal tools.\n\nKimi K3 also excels in tasks blending software engineering with visual reasoning — it leverages screenshots and visuals to optimize game dev, frontend, and CAD.\n\nThe case studies below show how Kimi K3's coding capability translates into open-ended software creation and scientific research.\n\n### Kernel Optimization\n\nWe tested the models' capability to optimize GPU kernels. Each model works independently in an identical sandbox, with up to 24 hours to profile, rewrite, and benchmark four tasks spanning AttnRes, KDA, and a 512-head-dimension MLA kernel across NVIDIA Hopper GPUs and GPGPU from an alternative vendor. Kimi K3 performed competitively with Fable 5 (with fallback) and substantially outperformed Opus 4.8, GPT 5.6 Sol, and GPT 5.5.\n\nClaude Fable 5 was evaluated by a third party, and its results may include fallback behavior. Across most models, some trajectories include small, acceptable precision shortcuts that remain within our numerical tolerance. GPGPU denotes general-purpose GPUs used for computation beyond graphics rendering.\n\nIn the late stages of Kimi K3 development, an early version of Kimi K3 handled the majority of the team's kernel optimization works.\n\n### GPU Compiler Development\n\n",

  "text": "GLM-5.2: Built for Long-Horizon Tasks\n\nWe're introducing GLM-5.2, our latest flagship model for long-horizon tasks. It marks a substantial leap in long-horizon task capability over its predecessor GLM-5.1 and, for the first time, delivers that capability on a **solid 1M-token context**. GLM-5.2's new capabilities include:\n\nSupporting long-horizon tasks starts with making long context engineering-usable: the model must maintain quality across long, messy coding-agent trajectories, not just accept more tokens. A 1M context is easy to claim, but much harder to keep reliable under real engineering pressure. To this end, we substantially expanded 1M-context training for coding-agent scenarios, covering large-scale implementation, automated research, performance optimization, and complex debugging. The result is a long-context system that is not only wide in scope, but solid in execution: a practical substrate for sustained engineering work.\n\nThis capability is reflected in GLM-5.2's performance on three long-horizon coding benchmarks. [FrontierSWE](https://www.frontierswe.com/) measures whether an agent can complete open-ended technical projects at the scale of hours to tens of hours, spanning systems optimization, large-scale code construction, and applied ML research. On this benchmark, GLM-5.2 trails Opus 4.8 by only 1%, while edging out GPT-5.5 by 1% and Opus 4.7 by 11%. On [PostTrainBench](https://posttrainbench.com/), where each agent is given an H100 GPU and evaluated by how much it can improve small models through post-training, GLM-5.2 outperforms both Opus 4.7 and GPT-5.5, ranking second only to Opus 4.8. On [SWE-Marathon](https://swe-marathon.vercel.app/), an ultra-long-horizon software engineering benchmark covering tasks such as building compilers, optimizing kernels, and developing production-grade services, GLM-5.2 still has room to grow, trailing Opus 4.8 by 13% while remaining second only to the Opus series. Across all three benchmarks, GLM-5.2 is the highest-ranked open-source model, showing that its 1M context has translated into practical long-horizon delivery capability.\n\nOn standard coding benchmarks, GLM-5.2 is the strongest open-source model, improving on GLM-5.1 by a wide margin: 81.0 vs. 63.5 on Terminal-Bench 2.1 and 62.1 vs. 58.4 on SWE-bench Pro. It also closes much of the gap to the closed-source frontier — on Terminal-Bench 2.1 (81.0) it lands within a few points of Claude Opus 4.8 (85.0) — while staying ahead of Gemini 3.1 Pro.\n\n",

  "code": "async () => {\n  // 读取 Kimi K3 博客页面的发布日期元数据（meta / JSON-LD / time 元素）\n  const metas = [...document.querySelectorAll(\"meta\")]\n    .map(m => [m.getAttribute(\"property\") || m.getAttribute(\"name\") || m.getAttribute(\"itemprop\"), m.getAttribute(\"content\")])\n    .filter(([k, v]) => k && v && /date|time|publish|author/i.test(k));\n  const lds = [...document.querySelectorAll('script[type=\"application/ld+json\"]')].map(s => s.textContent.replace(/\\s+/g, \" \").slice(0, 400));\n  const times = [...document.querySelectorAll(\"time\")].map(t => t.getAttribute(\"datetime\") + \" | \" + t.textContent.trim()).slice(0, 5);\n  const bodyDate = document.body.innerText.match(/(20\\d\\d[-年.]\\d{1,2}[-月.]\\d{1,2}|(January|February|March|April|May|June|July|August|September|October)\\s+\\d{1,2},?\\s+20\\d\\d)/g);\n  return { ok: true, title: document.title, metas, lds, times, bodyDate: (bodyDate || []).slice(0, 6) };\n}",

已完成核验与修订：你指出的两个问题都成立了，而且另外还发现 3 处日期偏差。

一、发布日期核验结果（原值 → 修订值）

模型上一版修订后依据
DeepSeek-V4-Pro2026-04-222026-04-24官方新闻页 publishedTime 2026-04-24，明确 V4-Pro 1.6T/49B 与 V4-Flash 284B/13B 同日开源；HF 4-22 只是建库[^1]
GLM-5.22026-06-162026-06-16（不变）z.ai 官方博客页标注 2026-06-16，与建库日一致[^2]
Kimi K32026-07-16（曾据评测日推断）2026-07-16（有官方依据）Kimi 官方博客索引页标注 2026-07-16；博客正文称完整权重 2026-07-27 前发布；HF 6-13 建库早于发布，不能当发布日期[^3][^4]
DeepSeek-V4-Flash-07312026-07-31未核实官方新闻索引最新仍是 4-24 的 V4 Preview，无该版公告；仅 HF 7-31 建库[^5]
Qwen3.8-27B2026-08-05未核实Qwen 官方研究索引无单独公告条目；仅 HF 8-05 建库[^6]
Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B2026-08-082026-08-03官方研究索引“Qwen3.8-Max：编程与办公，全面跃升”标注 2026/08/03（Open-Source）；HF 8-08[^7]
Qwen3.8-Flash-Next2026-08-242026-08-26官方研究索引“Qwen3.8-Flash-Next：全新架构…”标注 2026/08/26；HF 8-24[^7]
GLM-5.32026-08-252026-08-14z.ai 官方博客页标注 2026-08-14（“Today we are releasing GLM-5.3”）；HF 权重 8-25 才上线[^8]
DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash2026-09-10未核实官方新闻索引无对应条目；仅 HF 9-10 建库（10-17 另有 1017 修订版）[^9]

结论：HF 建库日与官方发布日的偏差最大达到 11 天（GLM-5.3）；对于缺少官方公告的 3 个条目，一律标“未核实”，只承认“不晚于 HF 上线日”。

三、基准不能直接横向比较（重要）

各家分数出自不同 agent harness、思考档位与上下文设定，不能当成同一把尺子：

  • Terminal-Bench / DeepSWE 类 agentic 基准受 harness 支配：Kimi K3 用 Kimi Code harness + max effort；DeepSeek 用自家 DSH 的 Minimal/Standard 模式（V4.1-Flash 卡还给出 Claude Code 69.8 vs mini-SWE 74.2 vs DSH Standard 70.5 的同模型差异）[^9]；Qwen 用 Claude Code harness，temp=1.0, top_p=0.95, 256K 上下文[^13]；GLM 用 300K 上下文上限、GPT-5.6-luna 作裁判[^8]。
  • 同一模型换版次分数就变：DeepSeek-V4-Pro 自己卡上 Codeforces 3206，而 V4.1-Flash 卡里的 DS-V4-Pro 列是 3348；GLM-5.2 的 TB2.1 在自身博客是 81.0，在 Kimi K3 对照表里是 82.7。
  • 对比表中的“第三方列”只能当参考：例如 GLM-5.3 表里 Kimi K3 的 DeepSWE 67.5 与 Kimi 自己卡一致，但 GPQA 是 92.9（Kimi 自报 93.5）。
  • 因此表的正确用法是“看同族趋势 + 看官方口径”，而不是跨行排名。

四、Data 表同步更新

表 开源大模型对比_2026H2 已重建为 10 列并全量覆盖：模型 / 发布方 / 发布日期 / 发布日期依据 / 参数量 / 上下文长度 / 协议 / 主要基准成绩 / 备注 / 官方来源，当前 9 行（9 条更新，revision=2）。每条都写明了“为什么是这个日期”（官方页面 publishedTime、官方索引日期，或“仅 HF 建库、未核实”）。GLM-5.2 的 TB3.0 4.6 已改为标注来自 GLM-5.3 官方对照表，其 GPQA 91.2 已改为引用自身模型卡（已核对列头为 GLM-5.2 | GLM-5.1 | Qwen3.7-Max | MiniMax M3 | DeepSeek-V4-Pro | Claude Opus 4.8 | GPT-5.5 | Gemini 3.1 Pro）。

  "content": "# 最近 6 个月开源权重大模型对比（2026-04-18 ~ 2026-10-18）\n\n数据口径：发布日期以**官方公告页**为准，无公告者标「未核实」并注明仅能确认的 HF 权重上线日（HF 建库日 ≠ 发布日，最大偏差 11 天）。参数量、上下文、协议取自各自官方模型卡/技术报告；基准分数为各家自报，评测设置不同，不可直接排名。\n\n## 总表\n\n| 模型（发布方） | 发布日期 | 参数量 | 上下文长度 | 协议 | 主要基准成绩 |\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n| DeepSeek-V4-Pro（DeepSeek） | 2026-04-24（官方新闻） | 1.6T 总参 / 49B 激活 | 1M（64K 原生 + YaRN×16） | MIT | MMLU-Pro 87.5；GPQA-D 90.1；LiveCodeBench 93.5；Codeforces 3206；MRCR 1M 83.5；TB2.1 87.9 |\n| GLM-5.2（Z.ai） | 2026-06-16（官方博客） | 753B（MoE） | 1M（1,048,576） | MIT | TB2.1 81.0；SWE-bench Pro 62.1；GPQA-D 91.2；DeepSWE 46.2；TB3.0 4.6（据 GLM-5.3 官方对照表） |\n| Kimi K3（月之暗面） | 2026-07-16（官方博客索引；权重 7-27 前发布） | 2.8T 总参 / 104B 激活（视觉编码器 401M） | 1M | Kimi K3 License | GPQA-D 93.5；HLE-Full 43.5（带工具 56.0）；TB2.1 88.3；DeepSWE 67.5；FrontierSWE 81.2；ProgramBench 77.8 |\n| DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731（DeepSeek） | 未核实（HF 2026-07-31；官方公告称已随 V4.1-Flash 退役） | 304B 总参 / 13B 激活 | 1M | MIT | TB2.1 82.7；DeepSWE 54.4；Toolathlon-Verified 70.3；NL2Repo 54.2；CyberGym 76.7 |\n| Qwen3.8-27B（阿里 Qwen） | 未核实（HF 2026-08-05） | 27.8B 稠密 | 262,144 → 可扩 1,000,000 | Apache-2.0 | TB2.1 73.0；SWE-bench Pro 61.7；DeepSWE 1.1 42.2；GPQA-D 89.2；OSWorld-Verified 84.3；AndroidWorld 81.9 |\n| Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B（阿里 Qwen） | 2026-08-03（官方研究索引） | 2.4T 总参 / 95B 激活 | 262,144 → 可扩 1,010,000 | Qwen3.8-Max 自定义 | TB2.1 86.6；SWE-bench Pro 67.7；DeepSWE 1.1 56.6；PaperBench 93.0；NL2Repo-Bench 55.9 |\n| Qwen3.8-Flash-Next（阿里 Qwen） | 2026-08-26（官方研究索引） | 125B 总参 / 6B 激活 + 51B N-gram 嵌入 | 262,144 → 可扩 1,000,000 | Qwen Community License 1.0 | DeepSWE 1.1 58.7；SWE-bench Pro 62.5；SWE-bench Multilingual 81.0；NL2Repo-Bench 48.1 |\n| GLM-5.3（Z.ai） | 2026-08-14（官方博客） | 753B（与 5.2 同基座） | 1M（1,048,576） | GLM-5.3 自定义 | TB2.1 88.2；TB3.0 28.3；DeepSWE 66.9；HLE w/ Tools 62.5；CyberGym 84.5；Agents' Last Exam 28.5 |\n| DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash（DeepSeek） | 2026-09-10（官方 API 新闻页） | 552B 主干 MoE（权重 763B）/ 8B 输入、16B 输出激活 | 1M | MIT | TB2.1 90.6；TB3.0 30.0；DeepSWE 74.2；GPQA-D 90.9；Codeforces 3471；CyberGym 88.1 |\n\n缩写：TB2.1/TB3.0 = Terminal-Bench 2.1 / 3.0；GPQA-D = GPQA Diamond。\n\n## 关键结论\n\n1. **1M 上下文已是旗舰默认**：DeepSeek V4 全系、Kimi K3、GLM-5.2/5.3 配置即 1M；Qwen3.8 系列保持 262K 原生、外扩到 1M。\n2. **协议三分**：DeepSeek 与 GLM-5.2 为 MIT，Qwen3.8-27B 为 Apache-2.0；Kimi K3、GLM-5.3、Qwen3.8-Max 改用自定义开放权重协议，商用需逐条看条款。\n3. **竞争重心转到 agentic 编码**：Terminal-Bench 2.1 从 81.0（GLM-5.2，6 月）升到 90.6（DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash，9 月）；DeepSWE 从 46.2 升到 74.2。同期 GPQA-D 仅在 89~93.5 波动，知识类已趋饱和。\n4. **参数规模两端分化**：一端是 27.8B 稠密（Apache-2.0、单机可跑），一端是 2.4T/2.8T 的 MoE，中间是 125B/6B 这种“小激活高吞吐”档。\n5. **迭代节奏极快**：DeepSeek 在 5 个月内走完 V4-Pro → V4-Flash-0731 → V4.1-Flash，官方公告称 2026-09-14 起把 `deepseek-v4-pro` 请求逐步路由到 V4.1-Flash，V4-Pro 已在退出通道。\n\n## 基准横向比较注意事项\n\n- **agent harness 不同**：Kimi K3 用 Kimi Code harness（max effort）；DeepSeek 用 DSH 的 Minimal/Standard 模式（V4.1-Flash 卡给出同模型 Claude Code 69.8 / mini-SWE 74.2 / DSH Standard 70.5 的差异）；Qwen 用 Claude Code harness（temp=1.0, top_p=0.95, 256K）；GLM 用 300K 上下文 + GPT-5.6-luna 裁判。\n- **同模型不同版次不同分**：DeepSeek-V4-Pro 自身卡 Codeforces 3206，V4.1 卡的 DS-V4-Pro 列为 3348；GLM-5.2 的 TB2.1 自身博客 81.0、Kimi K3 对照表 82.7。\n- **对照表列只能参考**：GLM-5.3 表里 Kimi K3 的 GPQA 为 92.9，Kimi 自己卡为 93.5。\n- 结论：适合看同族趋势与官方口径，不适合跨行排名。\n\n## 范围与排除\n\n- 未纳入：Meta（`meta-llama` 最新仍为 Llama 4，2025-04）、OpenAI（最新 gpt-oss，2025-08）窗口内无新开源权重；Google Gemma 4（2026-03-11）与 Mistral Medium 3.5 128B（2026-03-31）早于 6 个月窗口。\n- MiniMax-M3（2026-06，约 428B/23B 激活、1M、MiniMax Community 协议、技术报告 arXiv:2606.13392）基准仅以图片形式出现在模型卡，无文本分数，故不入表。\n- Kimi K2.6（官方博客 2026-04-20，1T/256K）刚入窗口，为保证表格聚焦旗舰未单列。\n\n## 数据产物\n\nData 表 `开源大模型对比_2026H2`：9 行 × 10 列（模型 / 发布方 / 发布日期 / 发布日期依据 / 参数量 / 上下文长度 / 协议 / 主要基准成绩 / 备注 / 官方来源），按「模型」去重。\n\n## 来源\n\n1. https://www.deepseek.com/en/news/v4-preview/\n2. 最近 6 个月开源权重大模型对比（2026-04-18 ~ 2026-10-18）

口径：发布日期以官方公告页为准；没有官方公告的条目标“未核实”，并注明只能确认的 HF（Hugging Face）权重上线日；Hugging Face 建库日不等于发布日。参数量、上下文长度、协议取自各自官方模型卡和技术报告。基准分数为各家自报，评测设置不同，不能直接横向排名。

总表

模型（发布方）发布日期参数量上下文长度协议主要基准成绩
DeepSeek-V4-Pro（DeepSeek）[^1][^2]2026-04-241.6T 总参 / 49B 激活1M（64K 原生 + YaRN×16）MITMMLU-Pro 87.5；GPQA-D 90.1；LiveCodeBench 93.5；Codeforces 3206；MRCR 1M 83.5；TB2.1 87.9
GLM-5.2（Z.ai）[^3][^4]2026-06-16753B（MoE）1M（1,048,576）MITTB2.1 81.0；SWE-bench Pro 62.1；GPQA-D 91.2；DeepSWE 46.2；TB3.0 4.6（据 GLM-5.3 官方对照表）
Kimi K3（月之暗面）[^5][^6][^7]2026-07-162.8T 总参 / 104B 激活（视觉编码器 401M）1MKimi K3 LicenseGPQA-D 93.5；HLE-Full 43.5（带工具 56.0）；TB2.1 88.3；DeepSWE 67.5；FrontierSWE 81.2；ProgramBench 77.8
DeepSeek-V4-Flash-0731（DeepSeek）[^8]未核实（权重 2026-07-31；官方称已随 V4.1-Flash 退役）304B 总参 / 13B 激活1MMITTB2.1 82.7；DeepSWE 54.4；Toolathlon-Verified 70.3；NL2Repo 54.2；CyberGym 76.7
Qwen3.8-27B（阿里 Qwen）[^9]未核实（权重 2026-08-05）27.8B 稠密262,144 → 可扩 1,000,000Apache-2.0TB2.1 73.0；SWE-bench Pro 61.7；DeepSWE 1.1 42.2；GPQA-D 89.2；OSWorld-Verified 84.3；AndroidWorld 81.9
Qwen3.8-2.4T-A95B（阿里 Qwen）[^10][^11]2026-08-032.4T 总参 / 95B 激活262,144 → 可扩 1,010,000Qwen3.8-Max 自定义TB2.1 86.6；SWE-bench Pro 67.7；DeepSWE 1.1 56.6；PaperBench 93.0；NL2Repo-Bench 55.9
Qwen3.8-Flash-Next（阿里 Qwen）[^10][^12]2026-08-26125B 总参 / 6B 激活 + 51B N-gram 嵌入262,144 → 可扩 1,000,000Qwen Community License 1.0DeepSWE 1.1 58.7；SWE-bench Pro 62.5；SWE-bench Multilingual 81.0；NL2Repo-Bench 48.1
GLM-5.3（Z.ai）[^13][^14]2026-08-14753B（与 5.2 同基座）1M（1,048,576）GLM-5.3 自定义TB2.1 88.2；TB3.0 28.3；DeepSWE 66.9；HLE w/ Tools 62.5；CyberGym 84.5；Agents' Last Exam 28.5
DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash（DeepSeek）[^15][^16]2026-09-10552B 主干 MoE（权重 763B）/ 8B 输入、16B 输出激活1MMITTB2.1 90.6；TB3.0 30.0；DeepSWE 74.2；GPQA-D 90.9；Codeforces 3471；CyberGym 88.1

缩写：TB2.1 / TB3.0 = Terminal-Bench 2.1 / 3.0；GPQA-D = GPQA Diamond。

关键结论

  1. 1M 上下文是旗舰默认：DeepSeek V4 全系、Kimi K3、GLM-5.2/5.3 原生配置 1M；Qwen3.8 系列保持 262K 原生、外扩到 1M。
  2. 协议分为三类：DeepSeek 与 GLM-5.2 放行到 MIT，Qwen3.8-27B 保持 Apache-2.0；Kimi K3、GLM-5.3、Qwen3.8-Max 用自定义开放权重协议，商用要逐条看条款。
  3. 竞争已转移到 agentic 编码能力：Terminal-Bench 2.1 从 81.0（GLM-5.2，6 月）升到 90.6（DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash，9 月），DeepSWE 从 46.2 升到 74.2；同期 GPQA-D 只在 89~93.5 间小幅波动，说明知识类基准接近饱和。
  4. 规模呈现两端分化：一端是 27.8B 稠密（Apache-2.0、单机可跑），一端是 2.4T、2.8T 的 MoE，中间是 125B/6B 这种“小激活高吞吐”档。
  5. 迭代速度极快：DeepSeek 五个月内从 V4-Pro 走到 V4.1-Flash，官方公告称 2026-09-14 起把 deepseek-v4-pro 请求逐步路由到 V4.1-Flash，V4-Pro 已进入退出流程[^15]。

基准横向比较须注意

  • 代理执行框架（harness）不同：Kimi K3 用 Kimi Code harness（max effort）；DeepSeek 用自家 DSH 的 Minimal/Standard 模式（V4.1-Flash 卡还列出同模型 Claude Code 69.8、mini-SWE 74.2、DSH Standard 70.5 的差异）；Qwen 用 Claude Code harness（temp=1.0, top_p=0.95, 256K）；GLM 用 300K 上下文、GPT-5.6-luna 裁判。
  • 同模型换版次分数就变：DeepSeek-V4-Pro 自身卡 Codeforces 3206，V4.1 卡的 DS-V4-Pro 列为 3348；GLM-5.2 的 TB2.1 在自身博客 81.0、在 Kimi K3 对照表 82.7。
  • 对照表中的列只能作为参考：GLM-5.3 表里 Kimi K3 的 GPQA 是 92.9，Kimi 自己卡是 93.5。
  • 因此本表适合看同代演进与官方口径，不适合跨行排名。

范围与排除

  • 未纳入：Meta（meta-llama 最新仍是 Llama 4，2025-04）、OpenAI（最新 gpt-oss，2025-08）窗口内无新开源权重；Google Gemma 4（2026-03-11）、Mistral Medium 3.5 128B（2026-03-31）早于窗口。
  • MiniMax-M3（2026-06，约 428B/23B 激活、1M、MiniMax Community，技术报告 arXiv:2606.13392）基准仅以图片形式在模型卡呈现，无文本分数，不入表。
  • Kimi K2.6（官方博客 2026-04-20，1T/256K）刚入窗口，为聚焦旗舰未单列。

产物

  • Data 表 开源大模型对比_2026H2：9 行 × 10 列（模型 / 发布方 / 发布日期 / 发布日期依据 / 参数量 / 上下文长度 / 协议 / 主要基准成绩 / 备注 / 官方来源），按「模型」去重，每行注明日期依据。
